# Skills · K · part 5

798 entries, kanmer-auto to karpathy-guidelines. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **kanmer-auto**: Run a durable goal controller over a frozen roster — one ticket, one explicit Kanmer group, one area, an explicit ticket list, or the prepared board — keeping a resumable run record on the host group while driving each roster ticket through its profile pipeline with independent review and exact-merge verification. Use for /goal, or when the user says "clear HZN-003", "work through 0.3.3", or "finish this epic". DO NOT USE FOR one phase of one ticket — use the phase skills directly. ([collisionengineers/kanmer](https://github.com/collisionengineers/kanmer/tree/HEAD/plugins/kanmer/skills/kanmer-auto))
- **kanmer-auto**: Autonomously clear one explicit Kanmer group (epic or horizon), preserving a durable, resumable run record on that group while driving eligible tickets through their profile pipelines up to a requested point. Use when the user says "clear HZN-003", "work through 0.3.3", or "finish this epic". DO NOT USE FOR a single ticket or an ungrouped area — use the phase skills directly, or create/select a group first. ([collisionengineers/kanmer-core035-protected-20260824t142522z-dd4f69a4](https://github.com/collisionengineers/kanmer-core035-protected-20260824t142522z-dd4f69a4/tree/HEAD/plugins/kanmer/skills/kanmer-auto) · 3 copies)
- **kanmer-closeout**: Close out a verified Done ticket or an explicitly retired post-merge verification failure — finalize proof and traceability, clean its Git workspace, then release. For a batch, census the immutable roster and remove shared Git while its manifest remains linked before releasing every member. Use after kanmer-verify's success or terminal-retirement handoff. DO NOT USE FOR deciding whether the work is good, verifying it, or inventing a failure disposition. ([collisionengineers/kanmer](https://github.com/collisionengineers/kanmer/tree/HEAD/plugins/kanmer/skills/kanmer-closeout))
- **kanmer-closeout**: Close out a verified Kanmer ticket — confirm proof.md is final, record its commits/PRs and deployment status, then remove the worktree, delete the branch, and release, so nothing stale accumulates. Runs after kanmer-verify has validated the merged result and moved the ticket to Done. Use when the user says "close out &lt;ID&gt;", "wrap up this ticket", or "clean up the worktree/branch". DO NOT USE FOR deciding whether the work is good (kanmer-review), verifying on merged main (kanmer-verify), or tickets whose PR hasn't merged yet. ([collisionengineers/kanmer-core035-protected-20260824t142522z-dd4f69a4](https://github.com/collisionengineers/kanmer-core035-protected-20260824t142522z-dd4f69a4/tree/HEAD/plugins/kanmer/skills/kanmer-closeout) · 3 copies)
- **kanmer-docs**: Author and link the repo's governing documents (PRD/FRD/ADR) under /docs/. Use to create or update a product/functional/architecture doc, to satisfy the "every ticket links or creates a governing doc" rule, or to lay down the /docs/ structure. DO NOT USE for per-ticket pipeline docs (research/plan/proof — those live in the ticket folder). ([collisionengineers/kanmer](https://github.com/collisionengineers/kanmer/tree/HEAD/plugins/kanmer/skills/kanmer-docs) · 4 copies)
- **kanmer-execute**: Implement one Kanmer ticket from its read-only execution packet — take its recorded worktree and branch, work only the bounded checklist, write the post-implementation report, and open the PR. Use when the user says "work on", "implement", "take" or "build" a ticket, or when a planned ticket is ready for implementation. DO NOT USE FOR planning (kanmer-plan — required first), reviewing the result (kanmer-review), or post-merge cleanup (kanmer-closeout). ([collisionengineers/kanmer-core035-protected-20260824t142522z-dd4f69a4](https://github.com/collisionengineers/kanmer-core035-protected-20260824t142522z-dd4f69a4/tree/HEAD/plugins/kanmer/skills/kanmer-execute) · 4 copies)
- **kanmer-groom**: Groom and triage a Kanmer board by actually fixing it — dedupe near-duplicate tickets, fill in missing areas and profiles, split oversized tickets, archive dead ones, chase stale taken tickets, and repair off-board statuses. Use when the user says "groom the backlog", "tidy the board", "triage", "clean up the tickets". DO NOT USE FOR reporting problems without changing anything (kanmer-report). - **kanmer-plan**: Plan a Kanmer ticket — turn the inputs its live gates require into a concrete plan and executable checklist. - **kanmer-review**: Independently review a Kanmer ticket's PR against its packet, plan, governing docs, current head SHA, checks, and review threads; write the versioned whole-file review attestation, disposition every finding, then merge only with authorization and move one stage to Verifying. - **kanxiang**: 看相分析技能。通过用户上传的人体部位图片，识别图片类型（面相/手相/体相/骨相），调用相应的相术规则知识库，结合图片内容进行专业分析。支持面相（五官、三庭、十二宫）、手相（掌线、指形、掌丘）、骨相（头骨、体骨、九骨）、体相（体型、体态、气质）的综合分析。基于《麻衣神相》《柳庄相法》《神相全编》《水镜神相》《冰鉴》等经典相书整理。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/tree/HEAD/skills/kanxiang) · 2 copies)
- **kanyu-bian-xing-pian**: 当用户问某条龙上的星峰是什么星、会剥换成什么穴，或拿一处怪形异穴（鹤爪、撒网、盖天旗、拖枪、梳齿之类）问书里怎么读，或问九星相变次序与「变与不变」时触发。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-bian-xing-pian))
- **kanyu-dao-zhang-shi-er-fa**: 当用户已经有穴场（圆晕、太极）的样子，想知道该用顺杖还是逆杖、该盖该粘还是该倚该撞时使用；按《葬法倒杖》的认太极与倒杖十二法给倾向，不接实地作业指导。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-dao-zhang-shi-er-fa))
- **kanyu-er-shi-si-sha-zang-fa**: 当用户手头已有一处穴场的确切形势（来龙急缓、突窟、左右情势），想知道该按二十四砂葬法里的哪一条来读、或想弄懂「擔傘」「騎龍」「牽弓」等条目是什么意思时使用；只按原文作形势归类与倾向，不接实地作业指导。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-er-shi-si-sha-zang-fa))
- **kanyu-hanlong-gan-zhi-long**: 当用户想从整体上分辨一片山水里哪条是干龙、哪条是枝龙，或问「这地方的大势怎么分、真龙落在哪一道」时使用；按《撼龍經》给干枝之辨、两水夹送与关峡护托的读法，只给倾向，不替用户定穴。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-hanlong-gan-zhi-long))
- **kanyu-hanlong-jiu-xing**: 当用户面对一座或几座山峰，想知道它像贪狼、巨门、禄存等九星里的哪一种，或想知道原书对这类星峰的吉凶怎么断时使用；按《撼龍經》的星形与断语给倾向，标明是书里的说法，不作命定承诺。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-hanlong-jiu-xing))
- **kanyu-hub**: 当用户问一处地、一座宅的形势（来龙去脉、龙穴砂水、朝向、水口、周边好不好），或问某个具体日子宜不宜做某件事（嫁娶、动土、出行、开市、安葬等）时使用。本入口把问题分到相地（葬書、撼龍經/疑龍經/葬法倒杖）与择日（欽定協紀辨方書）两支，并路由到对应的 kanyu-* 子 skill。象征层只给倾向、不给命中断语；缺具体形势或具体日期时先说缺什么；不接阴宅实地作业指导、不给医疗法律投资结论、不替用户拍板。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-hub))
- **kanyu-wei-long-pian**: 当用户问一条龙旁边贴身走的那些水、小山是什么，或问天池、夹辅、前后池、水口关锁怎么认，以及怎么从贴身侍卫推到外圈的垣城与垣关时触发。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-wei-long-pian))
- **kanyu-xieji-ben-yuan**: 当用户想弄懂择日为什么用干支、五行、方位来立例，或追问二十四山、正五行／双山／洪範五行、三合六合纳音纳甲怎么来的时触发；不用于直接排出某日某时的吉凶宜忌。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-ben-yuan))
- **kanyu-xieji-bian-e**: 当用户问某个择日说法到底站不站得住、是不是术士捏造、该不该信时触发——这一支只做辨正，把书里辨世俗术家之讹的态度如实传达。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-bian-e))
- **kanyu-xieji-fu-lu**: 当用户提起附錄里那些世俗相沿的择日条目——男女九宫、逐日人神、太白游方、长星短星、百忌日、洗头日、嫁娶周堂、五姓修宅——想知道它们怎么来、能不能用时触发。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-fu-lu))
- **kanyu-xieji-gong-gui**: 当用户问择日所依凭的历法常例——节气怎么定月建月将、昼夜时刻与昏旦中星怎么查、官方祀典与迎春春牛依什么规矩时触发。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-gong-gui))
- **kanyu-xieji-huang-dao-gui-ren**: 当用户问黄道黑道十二神（青龙明堂天刑朱雀金匮天德白虎玉堂天牢元武司命勾陈）、天乙贵人、天官贵人、福星贵人、喜神落在哪时、宜忌如何，或问择时怎么用贵人时触发；不用于年方禄马或建除月神。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-huang-dao-gui-ren))
- **kanyu-xieji-jian-chu**: 当用户问某月的建除十二神（建除满平定执破危成收开闭）是哪一天、各主吉凶宜忌，或问建日、破日为什么吉多凶多时触发；不用于年神、月德类或日家贵人禄马。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-jian-chu))
- **kanyu-xieji-li-cheng**: 当用户问某个神煞「怎么起例、从哪一干哪一支哪一节算起」，或要把年月日时的吉凶神煞汇成一张可查的表时使用；据《欽定協紀辨方書》卷九·立成给出起例类别与对应条目，只给方法与表，不给吉凶结论。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-li-cheng))
- **kanyu-xieji-li-yong-zao-ming**: 当用户想按造命之法为造葬或修方配一组年月日时——先补龙、再扶山相主、避煞取吉星——并问「怎么造个好命」时触发。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-li-yong-zao-ming))
- **kanyu-xieji-lu-ma**: 当用户问岁禄、飞天禄马、飞宫贵人、通天窍、走马六壬这些按年方逐月飞宫的禄马贵人吉方落在哪、宜忌如何，或问「禄马同到」是否可信时触发；不用于建除月神或时家黄黑道。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-lu-ma))
- **kanyu-xieji-ri-shen**: 当用户问三合、临日、驿马、劫煞、灾煞、月煞、月刑、月害、六合、五富、天仓、往亡、归忌、反支、四离四绝这类按月建逐月轮转、供择日取用的日辰神煞时触发；不用于年神方位或时家黄黑道贵人禄马。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-ri-shen))
- **kanyu-xieji-yi-ji**: 当用户问某个吉日凶日到底能不能用、吉神所宜遇凶煞要不要一概忌讳、吉凶轻重相胜怎么取舍时使用；据《欽定協紀辨方書》卷十·宜忌给出神煞宜忌与轻重等第，报宜忌必附起例来源与卷次依据。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-yi-ji))
- **kanyu-xieji-yi-li-nian-shen**: 当用户问某一年方位、年神（太岁、岁德、岁破、大将军、白虎、劫煞三煞等）落在哪、宜忌如何，或想弄清年神的起例来源时触发；不用于月、日、时一层的神煞与建除。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-yi-li-nian-shen))
- **kanyu-xieji-yong-shi**: 当用户问某件具体的事（嫁娶、安葬、出行、动土、开市等）该看哪些神、宜什么忌什么时使用；据《欽定協紀辨方書》卷十一·用事按御用六十七事与民用三十七事列出各事宜忌之神。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-yong-shi))
- **kanyu-xieji-yue-shen**: 当用户问天德、月德、天德合、月德合、月空、天道这类按月轮转的吉神落在哪、宜忌如何，或问月德与月空为何是对冲之神时触发；不用于建除十二神、年神或日家贵人禄马。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-yue-shen))
- **kanyu-xieji-zhi-sha**: 当用户问某个凶煞能不能制、怎么制、修方时如何补龙扶山把凶化吉，或要逐条查制煞之法时触发。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-xieji-zhi-sha))
- **kanyu-yilong-shang**: 用于用户手里有一条长山大脉或一处大形势，想知道主干（干龙）到底往哪走、干上哪里值得看、眼前那些又高又尖的漂亮山峰是不是障眼法时，按《疑龍經》上篇「干上寻龙」的口径做干枝分类与追龙判读。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-yilong-shang))
- **kanyu-yilong-shi-wen**: 当用户带着《疑龙经·十问》那类具体疑难来问——继嗣抱养之家算不算数、一穴如何分长中少三房、几处坟的盛衰怎么合看、阳宅与阴宅择穴大小怎么别、主山客山怎么分、一处形势是真是假、花穴假穴怎么认、枝干气脉谁盛谁衰、龙星如何博换——时触发。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-yilong-shi-wen))
- **kanyu-yilong-xia**: 用于用户已经认准一条真龙、正要找它的落穴处，却发现身边先有一堆贴身的小窝小坡，拿不准哪个是虚穴假穴、哪个才是真穴，以及乳头、钳口、平坡、水际这类穴形该怎么判时，按《疑龍經》下篇「真龙落穴与虚穴」的口径做虚实裁断。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-yilong-xia))
- **kanyu-yilong-zhong**: 用于用户在两水夹来的大形势里，想知道枝里哪一段藏着干、州县都会方州大邑落在哪一段，以及怎么分龙身的背面、认缠护与鬼山官星时，按《疑龍經》中篇「枝中干、州县都会」的口径做归属与背面判读。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-yilong-zhong))
- **kanyu-zangshu-cang-feng-de-shui**: 用于用户问「风水」这个词到底从哪来、什么叫藏风得水、得水和藏风哪个更要紧、外气内气是什么时。触发信号包括「什么叫风水」「风水之法得水为上藏风次之怎么讲」「气乘风则散界水则止什么意思」「外气横形内气止生」。不用于问某地的形势吉凶（转 kanyu-zangshu-xing-shi-pin-lei）、不问平洋与山垄之别（转 kanyu-zangshu-zhi-long-bian）、不做阳宅摆设与现场择地指导。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-zangshu-cang-feng-de-shui))
- **kanyu-zangshu-cheng-shengqi**: 用于用户想弄清「葬者乘生气」这句相地第一原理到底在说什么、生气是什么、生气与枯骨和后人怎么相应时。触发信号包括「乘生气是什么意思」「生气指什么」「本骸得气、遺體受廕怎么理解」「为什么相地第一句要讲气」。不用于某块地具体该怎么葬、不给吉凶断语、不做阴宅现场作业指导；问某地的形势吉凶请转 kanyu-zangshu-xing-shi-pin-lei 或 kanyu-zangshu-shi-lai-xing-zhi。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-zangshu-cheng-shengqi))
- **kanyu-zangshu-shen-fei-ze-xiang**: 用于用户拿着一个已经选好、已经葬或已经建起来的具体穴场，问「这块地哪里废坏了、哪些形势该择来用、哪些该避开、哪些算生气哪些算死气」时；触发信号包括「审其所废」「择其所相」「避其所害」「避死挨生」「这块地还能不能算真穴」。不用于还没有任何具体形势描述的空泛求断，也不接阴宅实地作业指导。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-zangshu-shen-fei-ze-xiang))
- **kanyu-zangshu-shi-lai-xing-zhi**: 用于用户问「势来形止」、千尺为势百尺为形、全气之地是什么、为什么要「原其起、乘其止」、势与形各看什么时。触发信号包括「势来形止什么意思」「千尺为势百尺为形怎么讲」「葬者原其起乘其止」「全气之地当葬其止」。不用于问「风水」定义（转 kanyu-zangshu-cang-feng-de-shui）、不问支垄之分（转 kanyu-zangshu-zhi-long-bian）、不问某地贵贱品类（转 kanyu-zangshu-xing-shi-pin-lei）、不做现场点穴与择地指导。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-zangshu-shi-lai-xing-zhi))
- **kanyu-zangshu-si-fa**: 用于用户看到「乘金相水、穴土印木」这四个字，问「四法各指什么、怎么用在一处穴上」「什么叫乘金、什么叫相水」时；触发信号包括「乘金相水」「穴土印木」「穴中证应」「土即中央」「金鱼界合」「蝉湖之砂、虾须之水」。只做文本层的四法释义与证应归类，不接阴宅实地作业指导。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-zangshu-si-fa))
- **kanyu-zangshu-xing-shi-pin-lei**: 用于用户问原书里势和形分哪些品类、哪些势是吉的哪些是凶的、「势如万马自天而下」这类句子怎么读、以及怎么把一处地形对到书里的品类时。触发信号包括「势如万马自天而下是什么意思」「形如覆舟、形如仰刀怎么讲」「形势品类有哪些」「势与形顺者吉逆者凶」。不用于问「风水」定义（转 kanyu-zangshu-cang-feng-de-shui）、不问支垄之分（转 kanyu-zangshu-zhi-long-bian）、不问势来形止的机制（转 kanyu-zangshu-shi-lai-xing-zhi）、不做现场点穴与择地指导。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-zangshu-xing-shi-pin-lei))
- **kanyu-zangshu-ze-te**: 用于用户面对一群并列的山（重岡疊阜、群城眾支），问「这群山里该取哪一座」「众山俱小或俱大时怎么取」「是不是最高、最显眼的那座才作数」时；触发信号包括「择其特」「大则特小、小则特大」「参形杂势、主客同情」。只做文本层的取舍归类，不接阴宅实地作业指导。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-zangshu-ze-te))
- **kanyu-zangshu-zhi-e-long-lu**: 用于用户分不清一处地是「支」（平洋、伏于地中者）还是「壟」（山垄、峙于地上者），或问「支葬其额还是垄葬其麓、我这块地该落在哪一头」时；触发信号包括「支葬其额」「垄葬其麓」「平夷如掌」「卜支如首、眩壟如點頭」「缓处何妨安绝顶」。只做文本层的支垄归类与落点位置判读，不接阴宅实地作业指导。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-zangshu-zhi-e-long-lu))
- **kanyu-zangshu-zhi-long-bian**: 用于用户问「支垄之辨」、平洋（支）与山垄（垄）该怎么分、平地没有山怎么看、山垄又该怎么认时。触发信号包括「支垄怎么区分」「平洋龙怎么看」「地贵平夷土贵有支什么意思」「地势原脉山势原骨」「观支之法隐隐隆隆」。不用于问「风水」的定义（转 kanyu-zangshu-cang-feng-de-shui）、不问「势来形止」（转 kanyu-zangshu-shi-lai-xing-zhi）、不做现场点穴与择地指导。 ([zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/kuatixironghe/tree/HEAD/skills/kanyu-zangshu-zhi-long-bian))

- **kaoyan-english-coach**: 考研英语一/二的词句解释、语法、阅读翻译与写作辅导。覆盖完形、新题型、批改和非官方估分；结合可核验真题提供新手证据链图解。明确处于考研英语学习语境的局部词句或语法短问也适用；普通生活翻译、日常邮件润色不触发。不要用于找整套试卷、跨题复测、套卷模考、跨科计划或最新政策。 ([yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin](https://github.com/yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/kaoyan-408/skills/kaoyan-english-coach) · ★38)

- **kaoyan-error-loop-coach**: 根据用户提供的多题或多次记录聚类错因、安排间隔复测，并判断延迟掌握证据。用户说“这些题总是错”“帮我整理错题”“什么时候复测”时使用；未点名且主要目的是一题实际过程首错时，408 交单题错因入口，其他学科交对应教练，不要无证据猜测根因。 ([yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin](https://github.com/yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/kaoyan-408/skills/kaoyan-error-loop-coach) · ★38)
- **kaoyan-fushi**: 模拟考研复试全流程，包括中英文自我介绍、专业课问答、导师常规问题与综合素质考察，并逐项点评。当用户提到"考研复试"、"复试模拟"、"调剂面试"、"中英文自我介绍"，或给出目标院校与专业时使用。 ([shreyvijayvargiya/cizle](https://github.com/shreyvijayvargiya/cizle/tree/HEAD/library/v4/skills/awesome-chinese-skills-kaoyan-fushi-3311c6) · ★2)
- **kaoyan-grading**: 交卷判分和试卷分析：用户说“交卷”“批改”“判分”“试卷分析”“看看我错在哪”“错题”时使用。答卷照片放在收件夹，不贴进对话；照片交给转录者（识图子代理或另一个模型）转成文字稿，看不清的地方先问本人；转录在跑的同时主会话写标准解答和评分点并用 SymPy 核结果；主会话按正常、宽松两种尺度判分并写“错在哪一步”；再交一次独立复判；最后出解析集和成绩报告。 ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/keienhrri-bit/kaoyan-study-skills/skills/kaoyan-grading) · ★1.6k · 2 copies)
- **kaoyan-kongzhi**: 考研自动控制原理错题分析。按 S1-S6 闭环分析错题：拆最小知识点、查错题账本、归因（含控制专属错误类型）、双分支判定（A真题错题相似题仅非真题 / B普通错题可含真题）、文件库相似题三阶梯（按相关性+难度分布）、按反馈更新掌握度（L0-L4）、归档更新账本。触发词："分析错题""自控错题""控制原理错题""根轨迹""频域""稳定性"，或贴出自控原理错题（题干+作答+参考答案）。 ([jinglinghong713-rgb/kaoyan-kongzhi](https://github.com/jinglinghong713-rgb/kaoyan-kongzhi/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **kaoyan-material-study-assistant**: 把用户提供的学习笔记整理为问答卡、摘要、提纲和练习。仅使用当前会话实际可读且有权使用的讲义、笔记或教材片段，练习注明原创；短笔记或少量问答卡也适用。不补造未提供材料，不用于普通生活文本排版、具体学科讲题、真题趋势分析或扩写成原作替代品。 ([yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin](https://github.com/yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/kaoyan-408/skills/kaoyan-material-study-assistant) · ★38)
- **kaoyan-math-coach**: 辅导考研数学一或数学二的高等数学、线性代数及数学一概率论，结合可核验真题做新手图文讲解、错误定位与原创训练；题面歧义会改变答案时先最小追问，用户要求计算工具核验时必须实际调用并引用输出，未调用只能标人工复核。用户说“数学一这题”“数学二怎么做”“画图讲清”“帮我验算”时使用；不要用于跨题错因聚类、找整套试卷、套卷模考、跨科计划或最新范围核验。 ([yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin](https://github.com/yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/kaoyan-408/skills/kaoyan-math-coach) · ★38)

- **kaoyan-official-info-researcher**: 联网核验考研当年大纲、报名、考试安排、政策与单项官方招生通知。用户问最新报名时间、考试科目场次或某份官方文件以哪个为准时使用；院校发现、完整招生调研、往年比较和网友评价由招生调研员处理，不替代学科教学与学习规划。 ([yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin](https://github.com/yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/kaoyan-408/skills/kaoyan-official-info-researcher) · ★38)
- **kaoyan-past-paper-analyst**: 分析用户提供或真题搜索器已核验可访问的考研真题样本，提炼覆盖、结构、难度信号和有限趋势。用户说“这几套卷怎么看”“两年真题能看出什么”“按题号做覆盖表”时使用；不要搜索来源、替代单题讲解、组织模考或把小样本写成稳定规律。 ([yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin](https://github.com/yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/kaoyan-408/skills/kaoyan-past-paper-analyst) · ★38)

- **kaoyan-plan**: 备考规划与阶段复盘：用户说“规划”“阶段复盘”“检查点”“周计划”“明天学什么”“今天怎么排”“计划要不要调”时使用（某件事做砸了要找原因，归 kaoyan-retro）。小问题读状态卡直接答，给两三个选项；阶段复盘和周计划：读状态卡 → 写两套方向不同的骨架 → 挑一套改成调整方案 → 可选一次审稿 → 排时间的地方让用户选 → 定稿写进“决定”。 ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/keienhrri-bit/kaoyan-study-skills/skills/kaoyan-plan) · ★1.6k · 2 copies)

- **kaoyan-progress-diagnostician**: 根据用户提供的 ProgressSnapshot 或等价记录诊断进度偏差、风险、证据质量和调整信号。用户说“最近怎么总完不成”“哪科在恶化”“为什么落后”时使用；不要直接重写完整计划、安排今晚任务或推断未提供的历史。 ([yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin](https://github.com/yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/kaoyan-408/skills/kaoyan-progress-diagnostician) · ★38)
- **kaoyan-registration**: 考研报名——全国硕士研究生招生考试报名：预报名、正式报名、网上确认流程，报考点选择、学历学籍校验不通过怎么办、照片材料、报名信息逐项核对。当用户说「考研报名要注意什么」「学籍校验不通过」「报考点怎么选」时使用。 ([po-et/workbuddy-skills](https://github.com/po-et/workbuddy-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kaoyan-registration) · ★4)
- **kaoyan-report**: 备考交付物的固定排版和写法：做答卷分析、成绩报告、复习规划、讲义、订正册、背诵卡等给学生看的 PDF 时使用（“出个报告”“出 PDF”“讲义排版舒服点”“和上次那份一样”）。Markdown → pandoc → KaTeX → Chrome 无头打印，构建后输出版面机检（没渲染的公式、空白页、溢出、缺图）和一张缩略图，不逐页看图；正文不放出处括号、文件名和模型口吻；成稿由主会话一次写成，外援文字不进成稿（约 5000 字以上、不需要新推导的长讲义可交 writer 子代理照底稿写）。 ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/keienhrri-bit/kaoyan-study-skills/skills/kaoyan-report) · ★1.6k)
- **kaoyan-report**: 备考交付物的固定排版和写法：做答卷分析、成绩报告、复习规划、讲义、订正册、背诵卡等给学生看的 PDF 时使用（“出个报告”“出 PDF”“讲义排版舒服点”“和上次那份一样”）。Markdown → pandoc → KaTeX → Chrome 无头打印，构建后输出版面机检（没渲染的公式、空白页、溢出、缺图）和一张缩略图，不逐页看图；正文不放出处括号、文件名和模型口吻；成稿由主会话一次写成，外援文字不进成稿（roles.long_writer 为 subagent 时，约 5000 字以上、无需新推导的长讲义可交 writer 子代理照底稿写）。 ([keienhrri-bit/exam-prep-skills](https://github.com/keienhrri-bit/exam-prep-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kaoyan-report) · ★1)
- **kaoyan-retro**: 纠错复盘：用户纠正了 AI 的错误、或一件事做砸之后，诊断根因并提议最小改动。用户说“复盘”“为什么又错了”“记一条教训”，或讲题、判分、规划被用户指出错误时使用。只诊断和提议，用户逐条批准后才改任何规则、skill 或脚本；唯一自动写的是教训表里的一行。 ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/keienhrri-bit/kaoyan-study-skills/skills/kaoyan-retro) · ★1.6k · 2 copies)
- **kaoyan-review-executor**: 把既有计划或用户已给出的本次目标、科目和时长展开为一次可立即执行的时间盒。用户说“今晚怎么学”“现在有 90 分钟”“中断后怎么接着做”时使用，即使一次涉及多科也由本 Skill 处理；不要用于跨周配额、阶段权衡、目标日期倒排或进度诊断。 ([yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin](https://github.com/yq6666-66/408-codex-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/kaoyan-408/skills/kaoyan-review-executor) · ★38)
- **kaoyan-setup**: 建备考工作区和配置文件：第一次用这套考研 skill、或用户说“建一个备考目录”“初始化工作区”“配置考试和科目”“这套 skill 怎么开始用”时使用。问清考试、科目和日期，复制工作区模板（目录结构、AGENTS.md、CLAUDE.md、状态卡、决定、教训表），写好 kaoyan.yaml。其他 kaoyan-* skill 找不到配置文件时也先转到这里。 ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/keienhrri-bit/kaoyan-study-skills/skills/kaoyan-setup) · ★1.6k · 2 copies)
- **kaoyan-tutor**: 备考讲题老师：用户学某科的章节或专题、要求讲解推导、做题和口述纠错、订正错题，或说“继续”“讲一下这道题”“我哪里错了”时使用。以教材、考纲和真题为依据，沿知识主线讲；做题时先让学生自己做，再指出错在哪一步；讲解给推导过程和依据，不只给结论。 ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/keienhrri-bit/kaoyan-study-skills/skills/kaoyan-tutor) · ★1.6k · 2 copies)

- **karpathy-discipline**: Enforces Karpathy's 4 LLM-coding principles (Think Before Coding, Simplicity First, Surgical Changes, Goal-Driven Execution). - **karpathy-extended**: Five behavioral guidelines that catch the most common LLM coding failure modes -- silently running with wrong assumptions, overcomplicating simple changes, making edits that drift outside the requested scope, chasing vague goals with no way to know when done, and stating unverified guesses with confident tone. Consult this proactively for any non-trivial coding task -- writing new code, reviewing a diff, refactoring, debugging, or planning multi-step work -- and whenever about to state a technical claim you haven't verified. - **karpathy-goal-driven-execution**: Drives implementation through verifiable success criteria — transform vague tasks into testable goals, write reproduction tests before fixes, loop until criteria pass, checkpoint after every significant step, and declare uncertainty explicitly rather than silently proceeding. ([paulpas/agent-skill-router](https://github.com/paulpas/agent-skill-router/tree/HEAD/skills/coding/karpathy-goal-driven-execution) · ★10)
- **karpathy-graph**: Build typed, provenance-tracked knowledge graphs from any context — documents, codebases, incidents, research — so one or many AI agents share durable memory instead of copying transcripts. Use when the user says "build a knowledge graph", "criar grafo", "map this codebase/domain", "graph these documents", "extract entities and relations", "ground this claim", "visualize the graph", or asks how facts, sources and experiments connect. Every claim carries a source, every merge is reversible, every answer cites edge ids. Writes to the given path, or to ./.karpathy-graphs/&lt;graph-name&gt;/ by default… ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/karpathy-graph) · ★18)
- **karpathy-graph-build**: Stage 3 of the karpathy-graph pipeline, and the end-to-end entry point. Create a graph, assemble validated writes with provenance, and produce the machine and human artifacts. Use when the user asks to build or create a knowledge graph from a context, to add facts to an existing graph, or when running the whole extract-resolve-assemble-query pipeline in one go. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/karpathy-graph/skills/karpathy-graph-build) · ★18)
- **karpathy-graph-evaluate**: Stage 5 of the karpathy-graph pipeline. Score graph quality across extraction, resolution, structure, query, and operations, and run a ratchet loop that keeps only changes which improve the metric. Use when the user asks how good the graph is, wants to tune extraction prompts or the ontology, asks to measure or improve graph quality, or wants to detect resolution regressions. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/karpathy-graph/skills/karpathy-graph-evaluate) · ★18)
- **karpathy-graph-extract**: Stage 1 of the karpathy-graph pipeline. Turn unstructured context — documents, source files, incident timelines, chat logs, research notes — into typed entities and subject-predicate-object relations that fit the graph ontology. Use when the user asks to extract entities, pull relations out of documents, map a codebase into a graph, or when building a knowledge graph and no nodes exist yet. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/karpathy-graph/skills/karpathy-graph-extract) · ★18)
- **karpathy-graph-query**: Stage 4 of the karpathy-graph pipeline. Answer multi-hop questions from a knowledge graph by retrieving a bounded subgraph with edge citations, and ground or refute claims against actual graph paths. Use when the user asks a question that spans several facts, asks how two things connect, asks to verify or ground a claim, or when an agent needs task-specific context from an existing graph. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/karpathy-graph/skills/karpathy-graph-query) · ★18)
- **karpathy-graph-resolve**: Stage 2 of the karpathy-graph pipeline. Collapse duplicate surface forms into canonical entities using context as evidence, reversibly and with a recorded rationale. Use after extraction when the graph contains aliases, abbreviations, or spelling variants of the same thing, when isolated-node count is high, or when the user asks to deduplicate or merge entities. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/karpathy-graph/skills/karpathy-graph-resolve) · ★18)
- **karpathy-graph-swarm**: Fan the karpathy-graph pipeline out across parallel agents that share the graph as memory instead of copying transcripts. Use when the context is too large for one pass — hundreds of documents, a whole codebase, many independent hypotheses — or when the user asks to parallelize graph construction, run a swarm, or use multiple agents to build a graph. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/karpathy-graph/skills/karpathy-graph-swarm) · ★18)
- **karpathy-graph-visualize**: Render a karpathy-graph for humans — an interactive HTML explorer, a Mermaid diagram for docs and PRs, and a health report — then explain what the picture actually shows. Use when the user asks to see, visualize, draw, or open the graph, wants a diagram for documentation, or asks what the graph looks like. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/karpathy-graph/skills/karpathy-graph-visualize) · ★18)
